【港交所：陸續推出更多人民幣計價貴金屬等商品投資產品】港交所董事總經理兼市場主管餘學勤表示，金管局與證監會去年共同制定固定收益及貨幣市場發展路線圖，當中很多細節經港交所落實。他提到今年8月推出5年期國債期貨，未平倉合約由推出首個星期約900張，增至目前近3000張，吸引來自中東、歐美、東南亞等地的市場參與者，反映是值得發展的方向。

他在財資市場高峰會上透露，預計明年初將推出以人民幣計價、在香港交收的黃金期貨合約，並陸續推出更多以人民幣計價貴金屬等商品的投資產品，以豐富人民幣計價資產選擇。

金管局助理總裁何漢傑在論壇上說，推動人民幣國際化最大挑戰是國際間依然習慣使用美元，企業亦缺乏相應財資管理與融資工具。他指出，部分銀行為大客戶提供定製化方案，認為銀行可以將這些方案轉化為現成產品，再推廣至其他客戶。

他又說，香港人民幣資金池約1.1萬億元，金管局早前將人民幣流動資金安排規模擴充至5000億元人民幣，即相當於存款基礎40-50%，認為能為銀行體系提供堅實後盾，滿足市場營運、貿易及投資等方面融資的流動性需求。

【Bank lobby warns UK windfall tax will push investment out of London】Senior global bank executives told the Andy Burnham government they would shift investment out of the UK if taxes on the sector rise, warning the move could further damage London’s status as a financial centre. The warning comes amid concern over a proposed windfall tax in next month’s budget; Chancellor John Healey is seeking revenue to offset the impact of higher borrowing costs from the Iran war. Rupee Steadies as Softer Oil, RBI Sales Support — The Indian rupee hovered around 95.6 per dollar, steadying after gaining for three consecutive sessions as softer oil prices and continued RBI intervention limited downside, while traders remained cautious ahead of developments on US-Iran diplomacy. ### 2026-09-23 11:47:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Zinc Declines — Zinc prices fell to around $3,880 per ton as a stronger US dollar weighed on the market. Hawkish Federal Reserve commentary boosted the dollar, making dollar-denominated commodities more expensive for global buyers. However, the downside was limited by tightening supply and lower Chinese production. China’s zinc output fell 1.8% year-on-year in August, marking its first decline in nearly a year and the lowest reading since May 2025, amid smelter maintenance, mining disruptions and negative spot treatment charges. Lower production was also recorded at major mines, including Antamina in Peru and Red Dog in Alaska, as producers work through lower-grade sections of their ore bodies. At the same time, LME inventories remain historically low, with physical tightness particularly acute in Western markets. Meanwhile, an industrial accident was reported at Korea Zinc’s Onsan smelter last week, though no production cut, maintenance shutdown or suspension of operations has been announced.

Rupiah Holds Near IDR 17,800 Ahead of BI Rate Call — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,800 per U.S. dollar on Wednesday, extending recent gains as traders awaited Bank Indonesia’s policy decision later today. Markets expect the central bank to leave its benchmark rate unchanged at 5.75% for a third straight month as policymakers balance currency stability against domestic growth amid heightened global macro pressures. The board has already delivered 100bps of cumulative tightening earlier this year, leaving less room for further aggressive hikes without weighing on economic activity. Meanwhile, falling oil prices eased concerns over imported inflation and fiscal strain, although domestic price pressures persisted after annual inflation accelerated to 3.19% in August, driven partly by higher food prices amid El Niño-related supply disruptions. However, a firm U.S. dollar capped the rupiah’s gains, with the dollar index holding near an eight-week high as hawkish Fed signals reinforced bets of another rate hike later this year.

【申萬宏源：下半年10年期美債收益率核心波動區間或在4.5%至5%】申萬宏源研究近日釋出研究報告表示，美元指數短期與油價正相關，供給衝擊階段避險與相對基本面優勢常使美元隨油價走強；如果美伊衝突擴大，則美元指數仍有上行壓力，緩和後核心波動區間或回到97—103。美元指數趨勢性走強通常發生在美聯儲持續加息階段，9月加息已落地，但若未來只是點狀加息而非趨勢性緊縮週期，美元趨勢升值風險仍相對可控。展望下半年，10年期美債收益率核心波動區間或在4.5%至5%，通脹預期與油價聯動是短期利率的最核心變數；樂觀情景下或回落至4.3%，悲觀情景下可能向5%以上突破。中期來看，美債期限溢價易上難下，美債風險已從週期性波動升級為美國財政可持續性擔憂、美聯儲“通脹黏性、就業疲弱、長債自動收緊”三重困境以及全球儲蓄迴圈失衡的“灰犀牛”。當前美股估值分位不低，衝擊越強、持續越久對股價傷害越大。

【申萬宏源：油價或將在2026年末至2027年初逐季下行至75美元附近】申萬宏源研究釋出研報表示，進入2026年下半年，全球資產定價的核心矛盾已由年初的AI敘事階段性切換至美伊衝突反覆與能源供給衝擊。中期選舉前美國政府“壓油價，緩通脹”的訴求往往會加強，但這一次約束更強，難度更大。特朗普已將停戰與油價回落錨定至中期選舉前後，並明確“中期選舉後油價可能下降”。供給上，低庫存、海峽通航不暢、煉廠彈性不足、汽柴油裂解價差走闊，期限結構維持顯著現貨溢價，與2018年相比，供給緩衝更弱、政治壓價更晚。申萬宏源判斷，中性假設下，美國中期選舉前，油價或維持高位、中樞約100美元/桶；中期選舉後，油價或將在2026年末至2027年初逐季下行至75美元附近，但受庫存與成品油瓶頸約束，中樞仍將高於衝突前。

### 2026-09-23 11:24:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Falls on Hawkish Fed Outlook — Silver slipped below $67 an ounce on Wednesday, giving up gains from the previous session as hawkish signals from Federal Reserve officials outweighed the impact of falling oil prices on the inflation and interest rate outlook. On Tuesday, Richmond Fed President Tom Barkin warned that inflationary shocks could take time to fade, while Boston Fed President Susan Collins said she supported last week’s rate hike amid concerns that future inflation could remain above the 2% target. Meanwhile, oil prices extended their decline for a sixth straight session after President Trump said US officials had a “very good” meeting with Iranian envoys, while Tehran said it could reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if the US eases military pressure and lifts its blockade of Iranian ports. Lower oil prices help reduce concerns over higher inflation and further rate hikes.

Taiwan Stocks Hit Record High as Tech Stocks Rally — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, rose 355 points, or 0.7%, to 48,255 in Wednesday morning trading, gaining for the sixth straight session and hitting a record high, mainly buoyed by gains in electronic technology amid optimism over AI infrastructure investment. New Zealand Dollar Dips to 11-Week Low — The New Zealand dollar slipped to $0.570 on Wednesday, erasing the previous session’s gains to hit an eleven-week low, as the US dollar strengthened amid hawkish Federal Reserve rhetoric. His comments followed those of other Fed officials who have broadened their concerns about persistent inflation, reinforcing the case for further policy tightening. This outweighed growing expectations on additional interest rate hike by the Reserve Bank of New Zealand. Markets have increased bets on a third rate hike to 3.0% at the RBNZ’s October meeting to 75%, after Governor Anna Breman warned that persistent increases in oil prices could push near-term inflation somewhat above the central bank’s latest forecast. However, her emphasis on an uneven economic recovery underscored the challenge of balancing inflation risks with still-fragile domestic growth.

【教育部：建立分類評價機制 推進各類高校特色發展】在國務院新聞辦公室今天（23日）舉行的“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會上，教育部發展規劃司司長郭鵬介紹，2025年，我國高等教育毛入學率達到61.3%，在學總規模達4873萬人，已實現從精英化、大眾化到普及化的歷史性跨越。面對世界上規模最大的高等教育體系，不能只有一個賽道、一種模式，需要透過分類施策，更好地發揮各類高校功能，更好適應經濟社會多樣化需求。分類推進高校改革，分類是方法，改革是動力，發展是目的。分類不是分層次，而是分賽道，大的方向是以研究型、應用型、技能型等基本辦學定位，綜合性、特色化基本方向，引導高校科學定位；透過完善差異化政策支援，推進理工農醫、人文社科、藝術體育、師範等各類高校特色發展，從而最佳化高校佈局結構和人才培養結構。這次改革在推動高校辦出特色的同時，特別強調貢獻導向，以此激發分類發展動能。研究型高校重點要支撐好國家重大戰略需求。在新一輪“雙一流”建設中，將進一步強化國家使命引領、戰略驅動的前瞻性佈局，引導研究型高校加強與國家戰略目標、戰略任務的對接，凝練重大科技問題和研究領域，加強基礎前沿探索和關鍵技術突破，打造服務國家重大戰略支撐力量。應用型高校重點要服務好區域高質量發展。教育部正在謀劃“雙優”建設計劃，以辦學能力優質、服務區域經濟社會發展優秀為目標，“十五五”期間，將支援建設200所左右高水平應用型本科高校、若干優質特色學科專業，打造應用基礎研究、技術創新和應用型創新人才培養高地，服務區域創新體系建設。技能型高校重點要緊密對接好生產實踐一線。推進“新雙高”建設計劃，引領帶動技能型高校發展，建強市域產教聯合體、行業產教融合共同體，緊密對接先進製造業、現代服務業中的重點領域，促進校地校企深度協同聯動，為產業一線輸送大批高技能人才。下一步，將強化政策供給，加快建立分類管理、分類評價機制，健全資源配置激勵機制，加快構建自強卓越的高等教育體系，更好地支撐和服務中國式現代化。

【翁傑明：建議為國資國企發展未來產業出臺專項支援制度】日前舉行的第十一屆（秋季）央企投資發展大會上，全國人大常委會委員、財政經濟委員會副主任委員翁傑明在主旨演講時表示，中央企業要成為未來產業主力軍，但當前國企現有體制機制難以適配未來產業高風險、長週期的發展特徵。翁傑明指出，未來產業具有戰略性、顛覆性、高度不確定性，建議將未來產業包括高水平新興產業單列，建立週期科學、股權多元、容錯試錯的投資“特區”，支援國資國企參與未來產業發展。具體來說，建議國資監管出臺專項規則，未來產業投資基金收益考核週期不低於10—15年，新興產業不少於5—7年；基金存續期內不考核單個專案成敗，以基金全週期全部專案綜合收益率評價投資成效。

Copper Hits All-Time High — Copper futures climbed toward $6.8 per pound on Wednesday, reaching fresh all-time highs as persistent supply concerns and strong demand continued to drive prices higher. Metals trader Sprott Asset Management said global mined copper production could decline this year for the first time since 2017 due to supply disruptions, declining ore grades and weak output from Chile. Disruptions at major mines in Indonesia and the Democratic Republic of Congo have also reduced expected annual production by an estimated 600,000 tons. On the demand side, consumption remains robust across power grids, AI data centers and the defense sector, while the recent rally in technology and AI stocks has further strengthened the demand outlook. Elsewhere, traders are monitoring US copper tariff plans, with the Commerce Department proposing duties of 15% from January 2027 and 30% from 2028, pending a presidential decision.

US Natgas Prices Hit 11-Week High — US natural gas prices climbed above $3.0/MMBtu, reaching their highest level since July 8, as forecasts for hot US autumn weather pointed to stronger demand for gas-fired power generation. Above-average temperatures are expected across the South-Central US through October 6, potentially extending air-conditioning use and lifting gas consumption. The storage surplus is also shrinking as late-summer heat boosted power-sector demand, offsetting the impact of strong production. Analysts estimated that inventories were 3% above the five-year average for the week ended September 18, down from a 3.7% surplus a week ago. At the same time, LNG export activity remains robust, with average gas flows to the nine major LNG export plants rising to 18 bcfd so far in September from 17.2 bcfd in August. Stronger overseas demand is also supporting exports, with buyers in Europe and Asia seeking to replenish inventories ahead of winter amid disruptions to LNG flows from the Persian Gulf.

Offshore Yuan Extends Decline — The offshore yuan weakened to around 6.70 per dollar on Wednesday, extending losses from the previous session as a firmer US dollar and caution ahead of the highly anticipated Trump-Xi summit weighed on sentiment. Pressure on the yuan was also driven by subdued optimism surrounding the summit after preliminary talks that held last Sunday failed to produce an agreement to extend the tariff truce due to expire in November. ### 2026-09-23 10:06:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Hong Kong #Stock Market #Importance 1

Hong Kong Stocks Retreat — The Hang Seng Index fell 0.9%, or 215 points, to 24,873 on Wednesday, reversing a modest gain in the previous session as investors took profits in technology stocks and remained cautious ahead of the expected meeting between Chinese President Xi Jinping and US President Donald Trump. The decline followed three consecutive sessions of gains, with technology shares leading the rebound. Investors are watching the talks for signals on trade, semiconductors, artificial intelligence and supply chains, with AI emerging as a key area of strategic competition between the world's two largest economies. Meanwhile, Brent crude fell below $100 a barrel, offering some relief from recent inflation concerns. Finance, technology services and retail trade stocks led the decline, with Tencent (-1.1%), AIA (-1.3%), Xiaomi (-1.0%) and Shanghai Iluvatar CoreX (-5.2%) among notable laggards. Alibaba also remained in focus after its September 23 AGM voting-results disclosure.

### 2026-09-23 10:01:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Stock Market #Importance 2

China Stocks Fall as Trump-Xi Summit Looms — The Shanghai Composite fell 0.2% to 3,944 on Wednesday, while the Shenzhen Component slipped 0.4% to 13,673, as investors remained cautious ahead of the highly anticipated Trump-Xi summit. As preliminary talks held in New York last Sunday failed to secure an agreement to extend the tariff truce set to expire in November, expectations for the Trump-Xi meeting remain limited. However, the summit is still expected to cover a range of sensitive issues, including artificial intelligence, Iran, Taiwan, and trade relations, as both sides seek to stabilize bilateral ties. Xi and his wife, Peng Liyuan, are scheduled to be welcomed by Trump on Wednesday evening, marking the Chinese president's first visit to the United States since 2015. Among notable decliners, Bank of China fell 0.5%, CNOOC lost 0.9%, Zhongji Innolight dropped 1.4%, and Suzhou TFC Optical Communication retreated 1.2%.

### 2026-09-23 10:00:39  #其他 #焦點

【區分藥用、非藥用！芬太尼類物質犯罪定罪量刑有新標準】為依法準確懲處芬太尼類物質犯罪，近日，最高人民法院、最高人民檢察院、公安部聯合印發了《關於辦理涉芬太尼類物質刑事案件適用法律等若干問題的意見》（法發〔2026〕12號）。《意見》嚴格依據刑法、刑事訴訟法等有關法律規定，對涉芬太尼類物質刑事案件適用法律等若干問題提出指導意見，強調依法從嚴懲治芬太尼類物質犯罪，堅決維護人民群眾生命健康和社會和諧穩定。《意見》共18條，主要明確了芬太尼類物質犯罪的定罪量刑數量標準，將芬太尼類物質分為藥用和非藥用兩類，藥用的芬太尼類物質參照《最高人民法院關於審理毒品犯罪案件適用法律若干問題的解釋》規定的有關芬太尼的數量標準，非藥用的芬太尼類物質參照刑法規定的有關海洛因的數量標準。《意見》還規定了芬太尼類物質的範圍、收集證據的基本要求、認定明知的基本規則、違法所得追繳、關聯案件管轄等內容，細化了公檢法三機關辦理涉芬太尼類物質刑事案件的工作職責，為執法司法辦案提供明確依據。

【Eight foreign ministers urge Israel to halt violations in Gaza】Sept 22 — Foreign ministers of Egypt, Qatar, Jordan, the UAE, Indonesia, Pakistan, Turkey and Saudi Arabia met during the 81st UN General Assembly general debate and expressed deep concern about the humanitarian disaster in Gaza. They urged Israel to immediately implement obligations in the first phase of the Gaza ceasefire agreement, including ensuring safe, unimpeded delivery of humanitarian aid and supplies; to stop all violations and any forced displacement of Palestinians; and to halt settlement expansion and hold settlers accountable for violence. South Korean Shares Rise on Chip Strength — The benchmark KOSPI edged higher to around 7,050 on Wednesday, extending gains for a fourth consecutive session as advances in AI-related and semiconductor stocks on Wall Street bolstered Korean chipmakers. The Nasdaq Composite closed at another record high, while Samsung Electronics and SK hynix rose more than 2% and 1%, respectively. Gains were also seen in SK Square (4.1%), LG Energy Solution (1.1%), SK Inc (2.0%), and SK Innovation (4.8%). Additionally, easing Middle East tensions lifted the broader market as US and Iranian officials held a three-hour meeting, raising hopes for diplomatic progress. Oil prices fell around 1%, easing concerns over energy costs and supply disruptions. 【國家稅務總局最新發票資料顯示 今年前8個月全國統一大市場建設穩步推進省際貿易銷售額穩步增長】國家稅務總局發票資料顯示，前8個月，全國省際貿易穩步增長，重點區域外銷輻射作用明顯，交通物流和資訊服務穩步增長，先進製造業省際流動加快。——全國省際貿易穩步增長，重點區域外銷輻射作用顯著。前8個月，全國省際貿易銷售額同比增長1.3%，佔全部銷售額的比重為41.4%，較去年同期提高0.5個百分點。全國31個省份中，有20個省份省際銷售額增速同比正增長，增長面達64.5%。重點區域的區域外銷售額增速較快，前8個月，京津冀、長三角地區的區域外銷售額同比分別增長1.6%和1.8%，均高於全國省際貿易銷售額增速。——交通物流和資訊服務跨省穩步增長，推動跨區域交易成本持續降低。發票資料顯示，前8個月，交通運輸倉儲和郵政業省際銷售額同比增長8.2%，特別是多式聯運省際銷售額同比增長9.5%，新模式新業態運輸承載能力逐步增強。全國跨省資訊互通加快發展。資料顯示，前8個月，全國資訊服務省際銷售額同比增長41.1%。——跨省異地銷售穩步增長，先進製造業省際流動加快。一是跨省異地銷售穩步增長。發票資料顯示，前8個月，全國跨省異地銷售的涉稅經營主體戶數同比增長10.7%，佔發生銷售行為戶數的比重為56.5%，較上年同期提高1.9個百分點。二是先進製造業省際流動加快。發票資料顯示，前8個月，積體電路、鋰離子電池、智慧車載、電子專用材料等先進製造領域積極參與國內大迴圈，省際銷售額同比分別增長59.3%、59.7%、39%和74.3%。

Tehran also reportedly signaled a willingness to reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if Washington eases military pressure and lifts its blockade. Rising numbers of LNG tankers and crude carriers using alternative routes have also alleviated some supply concerns. Heating Oil Falls to 2-Week Low — US heating oil prices fell below $4.90 per gallon, the lowest level in two weeks, as expectations of improved supply and progress in diplomatic talks eased immediate supply risks. Saudi Arabia restarted its East-West oil pipeline at a reduced rate after a drone-related shutdown, with Yanbu exports expected to resume. However, full flows could take six to eight weeks to restore as repairs continue. Meanwhile, Iran said it could reopen the Strait of Hormuz within a week if the US eases military pressure and lifts its blockade, while President Trump said US-Iranian officials held a “very productive” meeting. Nevertheless, heating oil demand could strengthen as winter approaches, while refinery maintenance may constrain distillate production. Russia is also set to extend diesel export restrictions through October, adding to already-tight global distillate supplies. Industry data showed distillate inventories fell by 2.2 million barrels in the week ended September 18.

【江蘇銀行參與2026深圳企業赴港上市專題交流會 助力企業跨境融資與全球化佈局】9月22日，在證券時報社主辦的“潮起香江 聚勢共贏——2026深圳企業赴港上市與基石投資者專題交流會”上，江蘇銀行總行跨境營銷部負責人楊宇出席並發表專題分享，聚焦企業在跨境融資、全球化佈局過程中普遍遇到的資金統籌、架構搭建、外匯合規等實操堵點，結合一線業務實踐，深度拆解跨境併購、海外建廠等真實落地案例。楊宇從交易架構設計、資金路徑規劃、匯率及合規風險管控、融資方案定製等維度，清晰展示了銀行機構可為內地企業搭建全週期跨境金融服務體系，融合“金融+非金融”一體化服務能力，以定製化金融方案助力企業穩步拓展全球市場。此次分享為現場逾百家深圳擬赴港上市企業提供了跨境金融實操參考，受到與會企業代表廣泛關注。本次活動由證券時報社主辦，深圳市金融穩定發展研究院、深圳上市公司協會、國際上市交流基地協辦，聚焦深圳擬赴港上市企業核心痛點與發展需求，暢通政策解讀、實操指導、資本對接全鏈條服務，搭建一站式、專業化的港股上市交流平臺。（CIS）

Australia Stocks Edge Higher — Australian shares rose 17 points or 0.2% to 8,775 on Wednesday morning, extending prior gains after Wall Street hovered near record highs overnight as oil prices eased on progress toward ending the war with Iran. Focus turned to Thursday’s summit between Trump and Xi Jinping, with hopes for signs of progress on trade disputes. However, caution followed flash PMI data showing private-sector activity in Australia grew at the slowest pace in three months, with services cooling and factory output plunging. RBA Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter further weighed on sentiment with a warning that rates may need to rise again if inflation pressures persist, ahead of next week’s policy meeting. Non-energy minerals, industrials, and consumer durables led gains, while communication and commercial services fell. BHP Group jumped 2.1% while Rio Tinto (1.0%) also advanced. Other major gainers were Northern Star Resources (4.0%), Evolution Mining (2.9%), PLS Group (2.8%), and Sandfire Resources (2.7%).

【Galaxy Securities: AI sector enters rational verification phase】Galaxy Securities said in a Sept. 23 report the Fed hiked 25bps to 3.75%–4.00% on Sept. 16, the first increase since July 2023. A systemic rise in risk-free rates is lifting discount rates beyond the policy rate and compressing equity P/Es, disproportionately hitting high-valuation growth stocks dependent on distant cash flows. The AI narrative remains strong, but pricing is shifting from long‑term visions to near‑term realization. On the first trading day after the hike, storage, CPUs, foundry and optical‑interconnect segments outperformed; strong current earnings and short investment payback make these compute‑hardware nodes less sensitive to higher rates. Valuation differentiation is accelerating: hardware with verified cash flows and earnings (advanced process, memory, optical interconnect, equipment and materials) is benefiting, while narrative‑driven, unprofitable names are being further squeezed.

Australian Dollar Remains Steady — The Australian dollar was little changed around $0.71, struggling to sustain its rebound from a recent four-week low as firmer US dollar offset bets of an RBA rate hike next week, which is now largely priced in. Governor Michele Bullock said upside inflation risks may be materializing, pointing to persistently high energy prices and continued excess demand in the domestic economy. Earlier, Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter also reiterated that interest rates might need to rise for a fourth time this year to curb inflation. Markets are now pricing a 95% chance of a 25bp hike to 4.60% on September 29, with rates seen peaking at 4.85% early next year. Meanwhile, the greenback remains firm as remarks from Fed officials in the wake of its policy decision have flagged the possibility of more rate hikes if inflation does not cool. Elsewhere, traders watched the upcoming US-China summit for signs of trade progress, which could support the Aussie given Australia’s strong trade ties with China.

Dollar Holds Gains on Hawkish Fed Remarks — The dollar index held around 100.5 on Wednesday, hovering at its highest levels in eight weeks as hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials bolstered bets for additional interest rate hikes this year. Richmond Fed President Tom Barkin warned on Tuesday that inflationary shocks could take time to fade, with a risk that elevated price pressures may become entrenched. Boston Fed President Susan Collins also said in a LinkedIn post that she supported last week’s rate hike amid concerns that future inflation could remain above the 2% target. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 54% probability of another Fed rate increase in October. ### 2026-09-23 09:00:16  #其他

【商務部、國家統計局和國家外匯局聯合釋出 《2025年度中國對外直接投資統計公報》】9月23日，商務部、國家統計局和國家外匯管理局聯合釋出《2025年度中國對外直接投資統計公報》（以下簡稱《公報》）。《公報》分中國對外直接投資綜述、中國對外直接投資流量存量、中國對世界主要經濟體的直接投資、對外直接投資者構成、對外直接投資企業構成、附表等六部分，全面反映2025年中國對外直接投資情況。對外投資主要呈現以下特點：

一是對外直接投資規模穩居全球前列，呈現多元化投資趨勢。2025年，中國對外直接投資流量2135.8億美元，比上年增長11.1%，對外投資存量3.4萬億美元，連續9年保持全球前三，佔全球投資的比重增加到7.4%。對外投資行業覆蓋國民經濟的18個行業門類，主要集中在租賃和商務服務、批發零售、製造和金融四個領域，近年來逐步向綠色低碳、數字經濟和綠色礦產等領域穩步拓展。

二是互利共贏成效顯著。截至2025年末，我國在境外設立企業5.8萬家，遍佈189個國家和地區，境外企業從業員工總數476.1萬人，其中僱用外方員工296.5萬人。中國企業在海外積極融入全球和區域經濟，不僅帶動了自身發展、提升了競爭力，也為東道國產業發展和全球產供鏈穩定安全作出了重要貢獻。

三是“一帶一路”合作成果豐碩。2025年，中國企業對共建“一帶一路”國家直接投資460.5億美元，佔當年對外投資流量的21.6%，在共建“一帶一路”國家設立境外企業數量2.2萬家，投資存量4072.5億美元。“一帶一路”倡議為中國企業對外投資搭建了廣闊平臺，實現了“共商、共建、共享”的良好格局。

ADB Raises South Korea's 2026 GDP Growth Forecast to 3.2% — The Asian Development Bank (ADB) on Wednesday raised its 2026 South Korea economic growth projection to 3.2% from 2.6% projected in July, due to strong exports and corporate earnings, supported by the artificial intelligence boom. However, the agency warned of potential risks, including rising geopolitical tensions, increased volatility in global financial markets, and additional US tariff hikes, which could affect exports and investment. The ADB also revised up its 2027 growth outlook by 0.3 percentage point to 2.3%. The ADB added that expansionary fiscal policy and robust corporate earnings are contributing to a gradual recovery in private consumption. On the inflation side, the ADB maintained its forecasts at 2.7% in 2026 and 2.2% in 2027. Previously, the Ministry of Finance and Economy revised up South Korea's GDP growth forecast by 1 percentage point to 3% in its second-half policy report.

【媒體：反內卷為的是告別內耗穩定預期】北京日報刊發國家發展改革委國家資訊中心經濟預測部袁劍琴文章稱，反內卷，直觀看是對市場機制的正本清源。價格機制是市場經濟的“訊號燈”，“內卷式”競爭看似讓消費者得了便宜，實則透支的是產品質量、勞動者權益和行業可持續發展的後勁，最終損害的是消費者的長遠利益。整治內卷，就是矯正失真的價格訊號，讓市場重新沿著質量、效率、創新的方向配置資源，讓“優勝劣汰”替代“劣幣驅逐良幣”。同時，反內卷與反壟斷協同發力，規範涉企行政執法專項行動也在深化推進，為的都是讓經營主體安心發展。市場經濟從來不怕競爭，怕的是失序的競爭。反內卷表面上糾的是競爭失序，深層次是發展方式之變、治理理念之變、市場預期之變。把競爭從成本的“窪地”引向價值的“高地”，讓企業敢投資、敢創新、敢吸納就業，中國經濟必將成色更足、韌性更強。

### 2026-09-23 08:42:14  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Market Themes #AI Revolution #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500 #Impact mixed

【Galaxy Securities: Fed 25bp Hike Raises Risk-Free Rate; AI Shifts to Near-Term Validation】Galaxy Securities research: The Fed hiked 25bp to 3.75%–4.00% on Sept. 16, the first increase since July 2023, lifting the systemic center of the risk-free rate beyond the policy rate. Higher discount rates compress equity multiples, hitting long-duration, high-valuation growth names most. The AI narrative remains robust, but pricing is shifting from long-term visions to near-term delivery and earnings verification. On the first trading day after the hike, compute-related sectors—storage, CPUs, foundries and optical interconnect—generally outperformed, driven by strong current profits and short payback periods that make them less rate-sensitive. Divergence continues: compute hardware with validated cash flows and earnings (advanced process nodes, memory, optical interconnect, equipment and materials) are benefiting as downside is largely priced in, while names reliant on distant narratives without earnings support are being de-rated. AI is entering a rational verification phase.

【銀河證券：美聯儲加息抬升無風險利率 AI步入理性驗證】銀河證券研報稱，美聯儲於9月16日加息25個基點至3.75%—4.00%，為2023年7月以來首次加息。分母端，無風險利率中樞系統性上移，且抬升的不只是政策利率。對權益資產而言，貼現率抬升首先壓縮的是市盈率，尤其是依賴遠期現金流的高估值成長股。分子端，AI敘事並未降溫，產業景氣仍高漲，只是定價邏輯從“遠期願景”切換到“當期兌現”。加息落地後首個交易日，儲存、CPU、代工、光互聯等算力產業鏈環節普遍佔優：當期盈利強勁、投資回報週期短，使其對分母端利率上行具備明顯鈍感。分化並未終結，定價框架正在切換。有當期現金流與業績驗證的算力硬體（先進製程、儲存、光互聯、裝置材料）受益於利空出盡；依賴遠期敘事、缺乏盈利支撐的標的則被持續擠出，AI行業步入理性驗證階段。

【私募信貸機構熱切希望分食摩根大通訊用卡業務蛋糕】據報道，摩根大通近期研究了一項方案，旨在化解其與航空、零售等信用卡合作方長期存在的分歧。該計劃或將為私募信貸開闢新的消費債權業務空間。數百萬消費者正在使用的聯名信用卡，長期以來都是大型商戶與銀行之間愛恨交織關係的核心。航空公司、百貨商場以及各類商戶依靠信用卡專案吸引客戶、提升使用者忠誠度並增加收入；而持卡人違約帶來的大部分風險則由銀行承擔。但雙方之間常常存在矛盾：商戶希望銀行放寬標準，批准更多未達到信貸稽核及其他要求的申請人。按交易金額計算，摩根大通是美國規模最大的信用卡髮卡機構。據知情人士透露，該行近期開始研究，能否由其他資金來源為部分被其拒批的信用卡申請提供授信。知情人士稱，摩根大通已向十餘家機構發出問詢，探討是否參與所謂二次複核申請業務，即由這些機構承接摩根大通拒批申請的授信風險。這類機構在幕後搭建信用卡業務體系，業務範圍通常遊離於大型銀行體系之外，負責提供客戶服務、籌措資金，完成信用卡放款業務。其中部分機構已接洽私募信貸公司，試探其是否願意為摩根大通的這類信貸提供資金支援。私募信貸機構從投資者手中募集資金後對外放貸，其服務物件大多達不到投資級債券標準；但如今這類機構正越來越多地佈局信用卡債務領域。

【亞太經合組織（APEC）第十二屆運輸部長會議在京召開】據中國交通報，9月22日，以“協同與創新：塑造亞太智慧交通新未來”為主題的亞太經合組織（APEC）第十二屆運輸部長會議在北京召開。中國交通運輸部部長劉偉主持會議並作交流發言。劉偉提出3點建議。一是推進交通網路互聯互通，共同開創亞太交通合作新局面。持續深化跨境鐵路、公路、海運、民航基礎設施佈局，簡化人員、車輛、貨物跨境流轉程式，打通亞太區域跨境運輸堵點卡點。二是強化智慧交通協同創新，共同培育亞太交通發展新動能。聚焦智慧公路、智慧港口、智慧航運、城市智慧交通等領域，共建示範合作專案、共享實踐應用案例、共築多邊合作平臺。三是深化多方務實合作，共同打造亞太交通共贏新生態。健全多層次、常態化的多邊合作機制，以智慧交通為引領，帶動亞太交通更寬領域、更深層次務實合作。

### 2026-09-23 08:24:49  #央行 #公司 #市場 #國際

【紐約聯儲：美國國債市場交易時段正逐漸向股市靠攏】美國國債市場正日益向股票市場的交易慣例靠攏，從而緩解了長期存在的定價時間錯配問題，而這一問題過去不時給基金經理帶來困擾。紐約聯邦儲備銀行最新研究顯示，過去幾年裡，紐約時間下午4點發生的美債交易佔比持續上升。2021年之後，這一趨勢進一步加快，當時彭博指數服務公司(Bloomberg Index Services Ltd)將其美國債券基準指數的收盤定價時間從紐約時間下午3點調整至下午4點。這一變化是債券市場逐步向股票市場靠攏的長期演變過程的一部分。美國股市交易時間為紐約時間上午9時30分至下午4時。與股票不同，美國國債屬於場外交易市場，並不存在交易所規定的收盤時間。但指數提供商的定價規則實際上能夠形成類似於股市收盤的交易機制。

【中國駐美國大使謝鋒人民日報刊文：推動中美建設性戰略穩定關係落地落實】中國駐美國大使謝鋒在人民日報發表署名文章。文章稱，把握主流，錨定合作為主的積極穩定。中美共同利益遠大於分歧，合作共贏是主流，更是大勢。雙方要落實好兩國元首重要共識，發揮好政治外交和經貿磋商兩大機制作用，拓展兩軍、執法、衛生、農業、旅遊、科技、人文等各領域交流，不斷拉長合作清單，壓縮問題清單，攜手多辦有利於兩國和世界的大事、好事，促進中美雙贏、世界共贏。

互學互鑑，塑造競爭有度的良性穩定。中美肯定有競爭，但應該是良性的，要在取長補短中共同進步，在你追我趕中共創未來，而不是在零和博弈中彼此消耗。人工智慧關乎全人類福祉，中美應該比的是怎樣更好驅動創新發展、確保安全可控、促進文明互鑑、完善全球治理，推動人工智慧向上向善、造福人類，不能再設“矽幕”，更不能搞人工智慧版“星球大戰”。


相互尊重，保持分歧可控的常態穩定。有分歧不可怕，關鍵在於建設性管控。中美數輪經貿磋商取得積極進展說明，只要雙方秉持平等、尊重、互惠的態度相向而行，就可以找到解決彼此關切的辦法。管控分歧重在誠信，談成了就要付諸行動，不能說一套做一套。美方應該停止炮製所謂“國家安全”“經濟失衡”“強迫勞動”等名目，對華濫施遏制打壓。

堅守底線，維護和平可期的持久穩定。中美合則兩利，鬥則俱傷。要跨越“修昔底德陷阱”，避免“大國政治的悲劇”，守住不衝突不對抗的底線。中國的主權、安全和發展利益不容侵犯，臺灣問題、民主人權、道路制度、發展權利四條紅線不容挑戰。中國堅持走和平發展道路，希望美方相向而行，以中美和平共處促進世界和平安寧。

元首外交是中美關係的“定盤星”“指南針”。2026年是中美關係“大年”，習近平主席對美國的國事訪問必將繼續引領中美關係巨輪破浪前行，行穩致遠。展望未來，我們期待在兩國元首掌舵領航下，中美共同推動建設性戰略穩定關係不斷落地落實，走出一條相互尊重、和平共處、合作共贏的正確相處之道。

South Korea Consumer Sentiment Recovers in September — South Korea’s Composite Consumer Sentiment Index (CCSI) rose 2.1 points to 106.6 in September 2026 from August. Consumer sentiment regarding current living standards was unchanged at 92, while sentiment about the future outlook rose one point to 98. Sentiment regarding future household income increased two points to 102, while that concerning future household spending rose one point to 110. Consumer sentiment about current domestic economic conditions climbed four points to 83, while sentiment regarding future domestic economic conditions rose one point to 90. Sentiment on prospective employment opportunities increased two points to 88, while expectations for interest rates rose three points to 128. Expected inflation over the next year was unchanged at 2.7%, while three- and five-year inflation expectations both remained at 2.6%.

### 2026-09-23 07:11:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Australia Factory Growth Turns Negative in September — The S&P Global Australia Manufacturing PMI fell to 49.3 in September 2026 from 52.0 in August, according to flash estimates, marking the first contraction since March. Manufacturing output fell to 46.4 from 49.6, the strongest decline in 21 months, as new orders weakened and export demand remained soft. New export orders declined for the fifth time in six months, while manufacturers reported greater supply-chain pressure as input delivery times lengthened again. On the pricing front, input cost inflation eased from August despite higher energy and fuel costs linked to the Middle East conflict, while output price inflation remained elevated. Business confidence also deteriorated to a three-month low amid concerns over economic conditions, costs, demand and customer retention.

【S&P GLOBAL MARKET INTELLIGENCE: Australian economy weakened at end-Q3; September momentum continued to slip】S&P GLOBAL MARKET INTELLIGENCE economist Eleanor Dennison said Australia’s economy weakened at the end of Q3, with growth momentum continuing to fade in September. The quarter remained slightly expansionary, an improvement on a weak Q2. Business sentiment stays positive but confidence in the growth outlook has weakened amid layoffs, rising cost pressures, softer demand and renewed export weakness. Services remain the main growth driver but the rebound there has softened; manufacturing is weak, with activity and sentiment indices turning negative for the first time since March. Commodity producers report increased supply-chain pressure, though inflationary pressure has eased.

New Zealand Stocks Trade Slightly Lower — The NZX 50 fell 27 points, or 0.2%, to 13,848 in Wednesday morning trade, but remained at its highest level since September 7, after gaining in the previous two sessions, weighed down by declines in healthcare, communication services, and real estate. Traders continued to assess a warning from RBNZ Governor Anna Breman that inflation could be higher if high oil prices persist. Caution was heightened as higher oil prices raised expectations of an interest rate hike at the upcoming meeting. However, further easing in oil prices capped the fall amid efforts to ease tensions in the Middle East. Optimism over the Trump-Xi summit later this week also prevented the index from falling deeper, as AI and tariffs are expected to be discussed at the meeting. Among the early losers were Fisher & Paykel (-1.4%), Chorus (-0.8%), Fletcher Building (-0.8%), Meridian Energy (-0.5%), EBOS Group (-0.4%), and Freightways Group (-0.4%).

【ECB Governing Council member Nagel: persistent high energy prices could push ECB into mildly restrictive territory】ECB Governing Council member and Bundesbank president Nagel said persistent high energy prices could force the ECB to raise rates to a level that suppresses growth, potentially moving policy into a mildly restrictive range. He added it is too early to draw conclusions. Nagel warned upcoming wage negotiations, including in Germany, could trigger second-round inflation effects, and the longer high energy prices persist the greater that risk. On the Transmission Protection Instrument (TPI), he said it can only be activated if monetary policy transmission is impaired and is not intended to address a euro-area country's fiscal problems.

### 2026-09-23 06:14:08  #市場 #央行 #國際

【交易員大舉對沖 以防美聯儲加息力度不及預期】交易員正在期權市場進行對沖，以防美聯儲加息幅度不及當前市場定價。利率互換目前顯示，市場預計到明年6月，美聯儲將加息三次、每次25個基點。上週，美聯儲決策者投票決定將基準利率上調25個基點，並暗示還需進一步加息以遏制通脹，此後市場對這一看法更加確信。這種鷹派的共識正促使一些交易員利用與有擔保隔夜融資利率(SOFR)掛鉤的期權來對沖押注。過去一週，與3月SOFR期貨掛鉤的看漲期權需求不斷增加，顯示投資者越來越希望防範美聯儲加息力度不及預期的風險。“市場定價反映的是從當前水平加息三次。我會持相反觀點，”Thornburg Investment Management固定收益主管Christian Hoffmann表示，“一年內加息四次，相對於當前的經濟背景而言是相當激進的反應，並將對宏觀經濟產生實質性影響。”截至週一收盤，2027年3月SOFR看漲期權的未平倉合約，也就是新增風險敞口，約為270萬張。這比同期限看跌期權多約100萬張，表明交易員傾向於防範屆時美聯儲政策路徑比目前定價更為溫和的風險。

【Iraq oil minister: August exports 70 mln barrels, northern route to expand】Iraq exported 70 mln barrels of oil in August and current daily exports exceed 3.0 mln bpd, Oil Minister Bassim Mohammed said on state TV. Baghdad has started a pilot to truck crude from southern fields to Kirkuk storage to raise supply into the northern export system and lift shipments via Turkey’s Ceyhan port to above 600k bpd. The initiative is aimed at boosting northern-route throughput after conflict involving the US, Israel and Iran disrupted Iraq’s shipments via the Strait of Hormuz. Kurdistan-region output is about 200k bpd.

### 2026-09-23 05:17:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Inventories Rise 1.79M Barrels — US crude oil inventories rose by 1.786 million barrels in the week ending September 18th, following a substantial 7.14 million-barrel increase in the previous week. Commercial crude oil inventories excluding the SPR have declined by 39 million barrels over the last 23 weeks, but remain nearly 12 million barrels higher for the year. For the week ending September 18th, another 400,000 barrels left the SPR to support commercial inventories, bringing total SPR holdings to 284.6 million barrels. The generally accepted operational minimum for SPR holdings is between 250 million and 300 million barrels. Cushing inventories rose by 2.082 million barrels, after falling by 246,000 barrels in the previous week. US production remained at 13.944 million bpd in the week ending September 11th, up 462,000 bpd from a year earlier. Gasoline inventories fell by 2.16 million barrels, while distillate inventories declined by 2.164 million barrels.

【US billionaire Boehly, backed by US government and Gulf capital, bids for Lukoil international assets】Todd Boehly, U.S. billionaire and former Chelsea chairman, is leading a bid for Russian oil company Lukoil’s international assets with backing from the U.S. government and Gulf capital reportedly linked to the Trump family. The assets, placed for sale after Lukoil faced U.S. sanctions last year, were written down to about $20 bln in March and span oil and gas fields from Central Asia to Mexico, thousands of service stations and substantial European refining capacity. Insiders say Boehly’s consortium includes the U.S. International Development Finance Corp. (DFC), which would take roughly a 15% stake, UAE presidential brother Sheikh Tahnoon and Qatar’s Al-Hayat family; Boehly has been preparing for months and seeks to displace U.S. buyout giant Carlyle, which had earlier agreed to acquire the assets. Boehly has not finalized terms with Lukoil; the proposal is advanced but the parties’ negotiation status is unclear.

【葉門政府軍稱打死打傷數十名胡塞武裝人員】亞丁訊息：葉門政府地方官員22日說，葉門政府軍與胡塞武裝當天在西南部塔伊茲省再次發生激烈交戰，造成至少16人死亡、數十人受傷。 葉門塔伊茲省西部沙馬亞塔因區區長阿卜杜勒阿齊茲·謝巴尼告訴新華社記者，政府軍當天在該地區擊退胡塞武裝的一次進攻，打死12名胡塞武裝人員，另外打傷數十人。交戰期間，政府軍還俘獲兩名胡塞武裝人員。 謝巴尼說，政府軍方面有4名士兵在交戰中身亡，另有數人受傷。交戰後，政府軍將胡塞武裝逐出沙馬亞塔因的一處關鍵區域。 此外，葉門政府軍發言人馬吉德·努扎伊利當天在社交媒體發表宣告說，政府軍空襲了塔伊茲省紅海港口城市穆哈以及葉門中部貝達省的胡塞武裝軍事裝備和人員。 另據胡塞武裝當天在該組織控制的馬西拉電視臺發表宣告說，該組織近期在葉門西部沿海地區，特別是塔伊茲省西南部和荷臺達省西部開展的軍事行動，已造成超過600名政府軍人員死傷、數百名政府軍人員被俘。胡塞武裝繳獲大量輕型、中型和重型武器。(新華社)

### 2026-09-23 01:14:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Tin Rebounds from 2-Month Low — Tin futures in the UK rose to $54,000 per tonne, rebounding from the over-two-month low of $51,880 on September 14th, as the recovery in the AI trade improved the outlook for global tin demand. Strong utilization rates from AI agents released by Meta drove equities for chip producers and datacenter developers to rally, lifting tin contracts due to their close cointeration this year. Tin consumption remained underpinned by electrification projects and AI infrastructure development due to the metal's broad utility in datacenters and battery storage facilities. The metal's soldering capabilities, which are useful in precision soldering in AI infrastructure, prompted industry players to signal that tin demand in AI servers should triple by 2030. Meanwhile, supply from major producer Indonesia remained low as Jakarta pulled back on the issuance of export licenses. On top of that, Jakarta further tightened mining permits and seized 500 tonnes of metal from mines without licences.

【美聯儲巴爾金警告：通脹壓力消退需要時間】里士滿聯邦儲備銀行行長巴爾金警告稱，通脹衝擊消退可能需要時間，且高企的壓力存在固化的風險。在週二為巴爾的摩一場活動準備的講話中，巴爾金詳細闡述了他對經濟和貨幣政策的最新展望。他表示，美聯儲上週的加息將有助於放緩通脹，但他並未暗示是否需要進一步收緊政策。相反，這位美聯儲官員明確指出，供應衝擊已不再是一次性或暫時性的，而是導致整個經濟體的物價壓力持續存在。“這些衝擊可能會隨時間消退，但我確實預計這需要時間，”巴爾金說。“在此期間，當前高企的通脹水平存在影響未來通脹的風險。”

Iron Ore Falls Approaches 1-Year Low — Iron ore futures in China dropped to under CNY 710 per tonne, approaching the one-year low of CNY 695 in early August as low demand and risks on financing trade. Iron ore trading giant Radiant world disclosed $870 million outstanding to six different trade financing firms, detailing the exposure of different creditors to the firm accused of fabricating invoices to secure funding, which has currently not been paid back. The crisis to the major trader halted a large source of physical iron ore liquidity. On top of halting a sector of the trading market, the developments are also due to tighten funding rules for other traders, having already been responsible for the sharp decline in ore futures in late July. Meanwhile, softening demand for construction and manufacturing in China also dented ferrous metal buying. New yuan loans were sharply under historical averages in August, and the construction PMI fell to a record low.

Gasoline Rises to Four-Month High — US gasoline futures held above $3.50 a gallon, its highest level since May 21st, following President Trump's remarks at the UN on the conflict with Iran. The US President stated that a deal with Iran could be reached with Tehran after the US midterm elections. Earlier in the session, Iran had proposed reopening shipping through the Strait of Hormuz within seven days, although contingent on the US lifting its blockade. Meanwhile, the crack spread rose to record highs amid widening refining margins due to rising refined fuel prices. As winter approaches and heating demand rises, refiners are shifting output toward higher-priced diesel, tightening gasoline markets. Persistent Ukrainian attacks on Russian refining infrastructure have also contributed to elevated prices, with several refineries either fully or partially shut down. As a result, Russia is set to extend its diesel export ban beyond September.

### 2026-09-23 00:08:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Edges Higher on Lower Oil Prices — The CAC 40 gained 0.2% to close at 8,155 on Tuesday, buoyed by a pullback in energy costs that eased concerns over inflationary pressures. Oil prices extended their losses ahead of potential US-Iran talks at this week’s UN meeting. Iran reportedly offered to reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if the US takes initial steps to ease military pressure. The pullback in the oil rally, which has fueled inflationary pressures and prompted major central banks to raise interest rates, lent support to equities. Luxury shares closed mostly higher amid lower risk aversion, with L’Oreal up 1.5%, Hermes rising 0.8%, and Kering adding 1.5%. Industrial stocks were mostly above the flatline, with STMicroelectronics jumping 4.4%, Air Liquide gaining 1.3%, and EssilorLuxottica rising 2.3%. Meanwhile, Orange tumbled 4.1% amid news of a stake acquisition in Biolevate. TotalEnergies lost 0.5% on lower oil prices. Financials also closed mostly lower.

FTSE MIB Edges Lower — The FTSE MIB edged down 0.5% to 52,096 on Tuesday, weighed down by heavyweight financials. The banking sector underperformed following a petition signed by major European banks, including Italy’s banking lobby ABI, opposing the EU’s proposal to address the concentration of sovereign exposures among banks. The ABI stated that the measure could negatively affect the public debt market, which is already under pressure from a global sell-off. Banco BPM, Unipol, Mediobanca, Banca Mediolanum, BPER and MPS all fell more than 1%. Poste Italiane fell 4%, leading losses on the index, following its bid for all ordinary shares of Telecom Italia (-3.3%). Utilities also underperformed as European gas prices remained elevated. Meanwhile, tech stocks STMicroelectronics (4.4%) and Prysmian (2.5%) extended gains from the previous session, although Technoprobe fell 0.6% following its positive debut on the index the previous day.

European Stocks Inch Higher — European stocks closed slightly higher on Tuesday, extending the sharp gains from the previous session with support from the tech sector and consumer staple companies. 【Paramount to launch $49bn debt sale after litigation clears】Bankers will begin marketing roughly $49bn of debt to back Paramount’s $110bn acquisition of Warner Bros. Discovery after litigation tied to the deal was settled, people familiar with the matter said. Bank of America, CITIGROUP and Apollo Global Management previously underwrote one of the largest leveraged buyout debt packages on record and drew strong investor interest before the deal was paused. With the transaction now cleared to close, bankers plan to start sales in the coming weeks. The financing package is split roughly into $30bn of investment-grade bonds, a $7.5bn investment-grade loan and about $12bn of subordinated bonds, with a broader investor target than a typical LBO, covering dollar and euro investors.

1、介紹任期內美國經濟、能源和投資處於前所未有水平：美國新增了超過100萬個就業崗位，國內貧困率降至歷史最低水平。股市創下了前所未有的水平。現在在美國投資的資金比在其他國家投資的任何時候都要多。在過去16個月中，沒有無證移民被允許進入美國。美國軍隊是世界上最強大的軍隊，過去12個月中投資了1.5萬億美元於美國軍隊。結束了8場持續了數十年的戰爭。
2、關於伊朗戰爭：敦促伊朗達成協議，絕不會允許伊朗擁有核武器。稱在伊朗問題上“有一個重大決定要做”。相信將在美國中期選舉後與伊朗達成協議。呼籲所有國家對伊朗實施經濟孤立。重大變化可能迅速發生，伊朗衝突結束後，油價將跌至衝突前水平以下。正在考慮兩種選擇，一個是與伊朗達成協議，或者把伊朗“送進地獄”。
3、關於俄烏衝突：俄烏和平協議也會達成，比大家預期的要更快結束。
4、關於古巴：古巴是一個“失敗的國家”，將會崩潰。國務卿魯比奧正在與古巴進行深入談判。正在尋求對古巴進行“根本性改變”。
5、關於格陵蘭島：格陵蘭島協議將使美國控制該地區，將在格陵蘭島建立兩個主要軍事基地，相關協議將於當天稍後簽署。禁止美國對手在格陵蘭島建立軍事存在或在未經我們批准的情況下進行敏感投資。
6、關於墨西哥：美墨關係取得了重大進展。
7、關於人工智慧：美國政府檔案今後將使用“超級智慧”（super intelligence）這一說法取代“人工智慧”（artificial intelligence）。反對任何試圖控制人工智慧的全球性“圖謀”。將鼓勵超級智慧，而非限制。

Brent Crude Edges Down — Brent crude fell to around $100 a barrel on Tuesday, extending losses into a fifth session, but remaining above a session low of $98.63, as traders continued to monitor diplomatic efforts to end the conflict with Iran. US President Trump told the UN that he believed the US would reach a deal with Iran right after the midterm elections in November. He noted that oil flows have increased since the conflict began, while suggesting prices could fall sharply once the war ends. Speculation about a meeting with Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian persisted, although Secretary of State Marco Rubio said he was unaware of plans. Japan’s Kyodo News reported earlier that Iran proposed reopening Hormuz within seven days if a US blockade were lifted. He also added that more oil is flowing than at any point since the war began and that prices will plummet once the conflict is over. Meanwhile, on the monetary policy front, Chicago Fed President Goolsbee said the central bank cannot overlook persistent supply shocks, while St. Louis Fed President Musalem said further rate increases may be needed to bring inflation back toward target. Markets currently expect at least one more rate hike this year.

### 2026-09-22 22:48:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Morocco #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Morocco Maintains Policy Rate at 2.25% — The National Bank of Morocco left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September 2026 meeting, marking a sixth consecutive hold and keeping borrowing costs at their lowest level since 2022. Policymakers stated that the impact of elevated energy prices remained limited, mitigated by subsidies on road transport and the continued maintenance of gas and electricity prices. As food prices declined, inflation remained low, averaging 0.3% during the first eight months of the year. Agricultural output is expected to rebound by 16% in 2026, while non-agricultural activity is forecast to slow to 3.1%. Looking ahead, inflation is expected to average 0.7% in 2026 before rising to 1.5% in 2027, driven by an acceleration in core inflation to 2.2% as the contraction in food prices is offset by higher imported inflation. Economic growth is expected to slow to 4.4% in 2026 and 2.9% in 2027, amid a projected 7.6% contraction in agricultural output.

US Fifth District Services Index Flat in September — The Richmond Fed Fifth District Service Sector Survey was flat in September 2026, with the revenues index rising to 0 from -8, while the demand index inched down to 2 from 3. Current business conditions improved, with the local business conditions index rising to -8 in September from -12 in August, while the future conditions index fell to 0 from 9. The current employment index fell to 2 in September from 8 in August, while the forward-looking employment index edged lower but remained positive. On prices, the average growth rate of prices paid decreased somewhat in September, while prices received increased a bit. Over the next 12 months, firms expected growth in prices paid to remain broadly stable, while anticipating a slight moderation in prices received. Looking ahead, firms remained relatively optimistic, as expectations for future revenues, demand and local conditions stayed firmly positive.

### 2026-09-22 22:18:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Richmond Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 2

US Fifth District Manufacturing Unexpectedly Declines — The Federal Reserve’s Fifth District manufacturing index fell to -2 in September 2026, marking its first negative reading in six months, down from 4 in August and contrasting with market expectations of an expansion in activity at 5. Both shipments and the volume of new orders fell into negative territory, to -5 and -6, respectively, while the order backlog deteriorated further, falling to -10 from -7. Meanwhile, the employment index rose to 7 from -2 in the previous month. Over the next six months, expectations for shipments (33 vs 26) and the volume of new orders (32) remained firmly in expansionary territory, although sentiment weakened for capital expenditures (-1 vs 4) and raw material inventories (-1 vs 3). Prices paid and received are expected to decline over the next 12 months, while employment expectations dropped notably to 8 from 20. Meanwhile, the future local business conditions index continued to decline, falling to 10 from 16.

【AI計費模式切換引企業不滿 亞馬遜微軟等以折扣挽留客戶】亞馬遜、微軟、Figma、Workday等軟體企業與雲服務商，正面向客戶及諮詢合作伙伴推出AI產品新折扣方案。客戶此前受定價模式變動困擾，對此已疲憊不堪。客戶此前上調預算採購Anthropic的Claude Code、OpenAI的Codex等產品，同時削減其他開支，來為這筆新增支出買單。如今企業在選購AI工具時變得更加謹慎。軟體廠商改為按照使用量或完成任務數量收取AI服務費，可能推高成本，不少企業對此頗有怨言。舉例來說，一家市值超400億美元的跨國製造企業高管表示：微軟調整GitHub Copilot這款程式碼助手的計費方式、改為按量收費後，向該公司提供了至少一個月的寬限期，企業在此期間仍可以按席位訂閱制付費。此次定價調整後，這家制造企業每月需要為員工使用該工具，額外向微軟支付約15萬美元。按量計費政策落地後，微軟還連續三個月，每月向該企業贈送數萬美元的免費額度。但這名高管稱，受此次計費變更影響，團隊正在考慮將程式碼開發工作遷移至Anthropic的Claude Code以及SpaceX旗下Cursor。

US Natgas Prices Edge Up on Tuesday — US natural gas prices fluctuated between $2.8 and $2.9 per million British thermal units as traders assessed production, storage and weather trends. Average Lower 48 output reached 113.0 billion cubic feet per day in September, above August’s monthly record of 112.2 bcfd, although daily production was expected to fall to an 11-week low of 109.8 bcfd on Tuesday. Strong output and mild spring weather kept inventories above the five-year average, but higher summer temperatures increased gas demand for power generation and reduced the surplus. Analysts estimated storage was 3% above normal for the week ended September 18, down from 3.7% previously. Weather forecasts now point to mostly normal conditions through October 7. Meanwhile, average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export plants rose to 18 bcfd in September from 17.2 bcfd in August, despite the temporary shutdown of the 0.8-bcfd Cove Point LNG facility for planned maintenance.

### 2026-09-22 21:47:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Coal Eases from 3-Month High — Thermal coal futures exported out of Australia fell to $145 per tonne from the three-month high of $149 on September 7th as higher output from China momentarily offset the recent jump in demand. Bodies operated by the Chinese central government issued a joint notice for local miners to maintain ample output after local prices climbed to multi-year highs. Domestic thermal coal production had fallen since May after an accident at the Shanxi province prompted a series of safety inspections in the sector. This coincided with strong demand among major consumers of Australian coal grades, including Japan and South Korea, as soaring prices for LNG drove utilities to increase capacity at coal power plants. LNG supply has remained at recent peaks as the war in the Middle East halted the flow of tankers from GCC countries. Consistently, the EIA revised forecasts to reflect a 1.2% increase in coal demand this year.

Policymakers also pointed out that economic activity has continued to moderate. Major banks posted losses, with Itaú and Bradesco down nearly 1.5%. Petrobras shed 1% on lower oil prices. Other notable laggards included Ambev (-1%) and Rede D’Or (-1%). Vale and Axia limited losses as Vale confirmed the acquisition of a 30% stake in Ligga and Axia approved a new redemption of Class C preferred shares. Other Fed officials, including St. Louis Fed President Alberto Musalem and Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee, continued to stress the need for additional tightening to contain inflation fueled by strong demand and rising energy prices. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 90% chance of another rate hike in December, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. ### 2026-09-22 21:25:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Nigeria #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Nigeria Delivers Massive Rate Cut — The Central Bank of Nigeria slashed its key interest rate by 350 bps to 23% during its September 2026 meeting, its biggest reduction since 2007, after leaving rates unchanged in May and July. Policymakers said the move was intended to restore the effectiveness of monetary policy transmission, as market rates had increasingly diverged from the benchmark lending rate. The Committee assessed that the current macroeconomic environment remains supportive of such an adjustment without undermining the disinflation process. The annual inflation rate eased marginally to 15.39% in August 2026 from 15.43% in July, marking the lowest since March and the third straight month of slowdown. Governor Olayemi Cardoso said inflation was expected to moderate further in the short to medium term, with previous tightening, exchange-rate stability and improved inflation expectations helping to sustain the trend.

【Meta帶火AI助手，但新產品已被亞馬遜封禁】Meta的最新AI代理Muse，正在海外成為現象級產品。第三方市場研究機構Apptopia資料顯示，Muse移動端目前的下載量，已經超過ChatGPT同期的下載量。上線12天內，Muse的全球安裝量達到280萬次，並迅速躍居美國蘋果應用商店第一名。這款9月8日上線的產品，本質上是一款可以替使用者“動手”的AI助手，核心能力是自主執行任務，包括訂票、處理郵件、呼叫Meta旗下社交媒體等。9月21日當天，Meta股價大漲11.43%，達到741.24美元，創一年多以來的最大單日漲幅，同時帶動與AI業務密切相關的半導體板塊上漲。這種增長速度被視為AI代理成為主流的訊號。據媒體報道，OpenAI也在加速開發面向消費者的AI代理，期望與Meta的Muse、馬斯克旗下的Grok Bot展開競爭。但在AI代理獲得消費市場青睞的同時，它與網際網路平臺的衝突也暴露出來。9月20日，亞馬遜封禁Muse的操作，使用者讓Muse在亞馬遜下單時會收到警告：“未經授權的AI代理違反亞馬遜使用條款。”（21財經）

【2026年全國檢驗檢測機構開放日活動在蘇州啟動】9月22日，以“檢驗檢測——賦能高質量發展”為主題的2026年全國檢驗檢測機構開放日主會場活動在江蘇省蘇州市正式啟動。同期，全國共有2663家檢驗檢測機構對外開放，其中603家為年度首次開放，154家國家質檢中心參與，預計受眾超10萬人次。本屆開放日的主會場活動，配套檢驗檢測行業與高階儀器儀表產業高質量發展展示，100家知名機構及企業集中亮相，覆蓋新能源、半導體、智慧網聯汽車、光伏儲能、生物醫藥、機器人等新興產業。20多個省（區、市）市場監管部門及轄區機構同步連線觀看主會場活動，遼寧、安徽、山東、重慶、四川與主會場影片連線開放現場情況。各地還將同步開展公益檢測與便民諮詢，推動檢驗檢測服務貼近產業與民生。檢驗檢測是重要的生產性服務業和高技術服務業，市場監管總局將2026年確定為認證認可檢驗檢測“規範與發展年”，全面實施“一單一庫”管理，印發《國家質檢中心提質最佳化三年行動方案》，引導行業從“事後把關”向“全程賦能”轉型。在此背景下，檢驗檢測機構正加速嵌入產業鏈供應鏈，從單一檢測向全鏈條技術解決方案升級；同時築牢公信力生命線，以“零容忍”整治違規，推動實驗室在陽光下執行。行業也正積極推動檢測國際互認，讓“中國檢測”成為跨國信任通用語言，並深化食品、環境、公共安全等領域的常態化惠民服務。

### 2026-09-22 20:34:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Futures Remain Above 18 Cents — US sugar futures traded above 18 cents per pound, holding close to their highest since April 2025, amid lingering concerns over the supply outlook. Weather disruptions in Asia and Europe are tightening the global outlook, with recent estimates pointing to a potential deficit in 2026/27. Thai sugar production is expected to fall by at least 17% to below 10 million tonnes, while below-normal monsoon rainfall in India is adding to concerns over output. The European Union Sugar Market Observatory also reported recently that the bloc's production in the 2026/27 crop year is expected to fall by 19%. El Niño poses an additional risk to global sugar production, with a strong event potentially bringing drier conditions to major producers. In top producer Brazil, uncertainty centers on how weather will affect the key Center-South region and the split of sugarcane between sugar and ethanol.

### 2026-09-22 20:23:48  #MKT News #DXY #Impact bullish #EURUSD #Impact bearish

【Ukraine drone strikes close Black Sea routes; Russia to export southern grain via Murmansk from next month using fertilizer-loading gear】Ukrainian drone attacks have left Black Sea shipping routes effectively closed, prompting Russia to begin routing grain from its south through the Arctic port of Murmansk as soon as next month. Murmansk will use equipment normally employed for fertilizer loading and a direct-transfer system that loads railcars straight into ship holds—technology typically used for potash—and is upgrading conveyor belts to handle larger grain volumes. The port is roughly 2,700 km from Russia’s main grain gateway Novorossiysk but can be accessed via neutral waters. Russian firms are converting fertilizer, coal and other bulk terminals in Baltic and Arctic ports, including Murmansk, to process grain exports.

### 2026-09-22 20:22:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Mexico Retail Sales Growth Slows in July — Mexico’s retail sales rose 1.8% year over year in July 2026, easing from a 2.7% increase in June. Growth was led by sales of healthcare articles (8.7%), followed by household appliances, computers, home décor and second-hand goods (8.4%), hardware, paint and glass products (6.6%), stationery, recreational goods and other personal-use items (3.1%), and textiles, accessories, footwear and clothing (2%). These gains more than offset declines in sales through online channels, printed catalogs, TV and similar platforms (-7.7%), breaking a multi-month streak of gains, as well as grocery items, food, beverages, ice and tobacco (-2.3%) and supermarkets and department stores (-0.6%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales edged 0.1% lower, following a 0.2% decline in June.

【伊朗擬重開霍爾木茲海峽 沙特重啟東西輸油管道 國際油價下跌】受重啟結束伊朗戰爭的外交努力及沙特關鍵管道重新開放的樂觀情緒影響，國際油價連續第五個交易日大幅下跌，創下兩週以來的最低水平。布倫特原油價格短暫跌破每桶98美元，美國原油價格跌破每桶93美元，均創下自9月8日以來的最低價格。報道稱，如果美國採取措施減輕對伊朗的壓力，伊朗願意在幾天內重新開放霍爾木茲海峽，受此訊息影響，油價隔夜開始首次下滑。伊朗半官方法爾斯通訊社稱“伊朗訊息人士將這些報道描述為不可靠和不實”，但未透露訊息人士的具體身份。這是華盛頓與德黑蘭可能重新就霍爾木茲海峽對峙問題展開接觸的最新跡象，對於渴望中東能源供應恢復的市場而言，這種可能性將帶來可喜的緩解。週二，美國總統唐納德·特朗普在紐約舉行的聯合國大會期間與多位世界領導人舉行會晤。特朗普釋放出願意與伊朗總統馬蘇德·佩澤希齊揚會晤的訊號，這已經緩解了一些市場擔憂。當被問及兩國領導人是否可能舉行會晤時，國務卿馬爾科·魯比奧表示：“我認為目前還沒有安排任何會晤。”魯比奧補充表示，美國對此類會晤“持開放態度”。自2月28日美國和以色列發動伊朗戰爭以來，霍爾木茲海峽的船隻通行有時幾乎陷入停滯。據MarineTraffic資料顯示，在過去一週裡，每日通行船隻數量從未超過20艘。

### 2026-09-22 20:18:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Hungary Holds Rate as Expected — The National Bank of Hungary left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 5.5% at its September 2026 meeting, as expected. The decision followed four rate cuts this year and came amid elevated global risks, with geopolitical tensions continuing to pressure energy prices. Headline inflation edged up to 1.3% in August from 1.2% in the previous month, remaining below the central bank’s 2%-4% target range, while gains in the forint helped lower import costs and improve the inflation outlook. Meanwhile, the forint appreciated 5.8% against the euro this year, supported by a pledge from Prime Minister Magyar to cut the budget deficit and to put the country on a path toward adopting the euro.

【SAMR and MEE offices launch baseline review of emissions measurement at key emitters in China’s national carbon market】The general offices of the State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) and the Ministry of Ecology and Environment (MEE) issued a joint notice launching a baseline carbon emissions measurement review of key emitters covered by China’s national carbon market. The exercise, aimed at implementing Party Central Committee and State Council directives, is intended to provide a clear roadmap for emitters’ carbon measurement management and to strengthen data safeguards for fair trading. 【報道：韓國選定得州電廠作為首個美國投資專案】議會訊息人士週二透露，韓國選定在美國得克薩斯州建設一座大型燃氣發電廠，作為其承諾向美國投入3500億美元投資框架下的首個落地專案。訊息人士稱，經過數月談判落實韓美聯合情況說明書（JFS），韓國產業通商資源部在閉門會議上向國會貿易委員會彙報了該方案。這份聯合檔案以韓國承諾赴美投資，換取美國下調進口關稅。據介紹，韓國產業部擬定的第二、第三項投資計劃，分別是在美國建設8座大型核反應堆，以及阿拉斯加液化天然氣專案。委員會官員表示，這座得州電廠選址恩西納爾市，總裝機容量6.3吉瓦。當前得州大量新建半導體工廠與AI資料中心，電力需求持續攀升。該專案投資額223億美元。韓國政府預估，未來20年專案最高可創造454億美元收入。與首個專案不同，第二、第三項專案仍需與美方進一步磋商。有報道稱，韓方希望收購美國能源企業西屋電氣約20%股份，用於建設8座核反應堆，但美方對此持反對態度。

【四川力爭2030年核產業規模突破2000億元】9月22日，在四川省綿陽市舉行的2026核技術應用暨核醫療產業創新發展大會上，四川省釋出涉及核科技與產業高質量發展、可控核聚變產業中長期發展規劃、核醫療產業發展“十五五”規劃等一系列檔案，其中提出到2030年全省核產業規模突破2000億元。四川是核工業與核技術利用大省，目前已構建起“核素—核藥—核醫療裝備—臨床應用”全產業鏈。“我們探索了‘創新平臺遴選轉化+科創投公司投早投小’成果轉化模式，已在綿陽轉化核醫療科研成果52項、實現投產27項，7個產品獲批國省首臺套（首批次）產品。”綿陽科技城管委會副主任韓曉清說。系列檔案提出，到2030年，四川將培育2家以上百億級龍頭企業，核醫療產業直接經濟產值突破300億元；圍繞核聚變能，加快關鍵核心技術攻關和非發電場景示範應用，建設國內一流的可控核聚變產業聚集區。（新華社）

### 2026-09-22 19:42:33  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【European gas falls to September low on hopes of eased Strait of Hormuz tensions】Dutch TTF and UK NBP wholesale gas prices fell more than 6% to three-week lows on Tuesday. The move followed Iran saying it could quickly reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the United States eases military pressure. Prices had already retreated on Monday amid renewed diplomatic expectations, leaving a roughly 12% week-to-date decline. Rasmussen, chief analyst at a global risk firm, said markets are focused on an upcoming UN meeting and the prospect of US‑Iran talks, but added direct talks are unconfirmed and any deal would likely require substantial US concessions that may be politically difficult ahead of the election. A trader said weather and Asian demand will be the key drivers over the next month, with the threshold for demand disruption likely to determine price direction.

### 2026-09-22 19:32:59  #宏觀 #焦點

【李強在上海調研時強調 加快培育壯大新興產業和未來產業 持續做大做強先進製造業】中共中央政治局常委、國務院總理李強9月22日在上海調研。他強調，要深入貫徹落實習近平總書記關於發展先進製造業的重要指示和全國先進製造業大會精神，堅持智慧化、綠色化、融合化方向，推進科技創新和產業創新深度融合，大力發展新一代智慧製造，加快培育壯大新興產業和未來產業，持續做大做強先進製造業。

在上海電氣國家人工智慧應用中試基地，李強察看專用機器人、工業機器人產品及其在複雜場景中的應用展示，聽取“人工智慧+”整合服務商、人工智慧原生工廠建設情況彙報，參觀智慧機器人裝配除錯中試車間。李強指出，人工智慧等新興產業發展日新月異，相關技術應用正在深刻重塑製造業技術體系、生產模式和產業形態，為製造業科技創新與品牌提升開啟了全新空間。要將我國製造業和數智技術兩方面優勢結合起來，縱深推進“人工智慧+製造”，推動人工智慧更好賦能先進製造業發展，促進研發、生產、管理等全流程智慧升級，進一步啟用資料要素潛能，帶動更多行業全面數智化轉型，打造更加智慧的產業鏈供應鏈。要瞄準製造領域共性難題，圍繞工業設計、中試驗證、智慧運維等關鍵環節，打造更多製造業生產服務平臺，強化整合服務和多樣化解決方案供給，助力各類企業特別是中小企業更加高效、低成本開展智慧化改造，不斷增強發展動力和競爭優勢。

上海未來啟點社群是一個專注於未來產業領域的科創孵化平臺。李強來到這裡，詳細瞭解社群創新生態建設、上海未來產業基金執行等情況，察看腦機介面、量子計算等領域技術產品，與科創人員、社群工作人員親切交流。李強指出，未來產業是先進製造業發展的潛在藍海。要加強對未來技術發展的跟蹤分析和研判，強化關鍵核心技術攻關，築牢基礎研究支撐，鼓勵大膽探索、包容試錯，不斷拓展產業創新前沿。要構建一流創新生態，推動產學研用深度融合，做強科技服務，積極培育和開放應用場景。針對未來產業成長週期長、市場風險大的特點，要大力發展創業投資，壯大耐心資本，建立未來產業投入增長和風險分擔機制。李強強調，人才是第一資源，擁有人才才能贏得未來。要強化科研機構、創新平臺、企業、科技計劃的人才集聚培養功能，探索拔尖創新人才培養新模式，努力造就更多高素質人才。（新華社）

### 2026-09-22 19:24:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Hold Near Records — US equity futures were muted on Tuesday, holding gains from the previous session amid softer borrowing costs for the corporate sector and new optimism on returns on AI agents. Contracts for the S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 were flat, with the former two hovering less than 0.5% from their record highs. Stocks were broadly higher premarket as oil and fuel prices softened for a fifth session following signs that Iran, the GCC, and the US could chase diplomacy to restore some oil trade in the Persian Gulf, trimming the surge in bond yields this month. AI hyperscalers, which have issued record levels of corporate debt despite high borrowing costs, were higher with Microsoft, Oracle, and Alphabet adding 1%. Banks and industrials also gained. In turn, chip producers inched down but still held most of their surge yesterday, buoyed by positive responses from Meta's Muse AI agent. AMD, Micron, and Intel inched down 1%.

【Iran signals possible Strait of Hormuz reopening; euro-area bond yields fall】Iran signaled it could reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the US eases military pressure, prompting oil prices to fall and pushing euro-area bond yields lower for a second session to near two-week lows. Germany's 10-year bund yield, the euro-area benchmark, briefly rose 4bps at the open then fell 1bp to 3.441%. On Monday it had dropped 7bps to its lowest level since Sep 10. Yields had risen in recent weeks as markets priced further central bank tightening to counter energy-driven inflation; traders now price about 35bps more ECB tightening this year, down from 40bps expected on Friday. Rabobank senior rates strategist Lyn Graham-Taylor said Iran comments drove oil down, weighing on yields.

Sensex Finishes Lower — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.4% down at 74,529.1 on Tuesday, after a choppy session, reversing the prior's day advance. Weakness in financials and tech stocks weighed, despite some relief from the pullback in oil prices. Meanwhile, investors were watching for possible diplomatic engagement between the US and Iran at the United Nations General Assembly this week. US President Trump is set to speak later today and said he was open to meeting Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian. Traders also eyed the National Stock Exchange IPO, which drew over $10 billion in bids and was subscribed 5.71 times, with shares set to debut on Thursday. Bajaj Finserv (-1.6%), Bajaj Finance (-1.5%), Trent (-1.5%), Sun Pharma (-1.3%) and TCS (-1.1%) were the top losers. Infosys (-1%), HCL Tech (-0.8%) and Tech Mahindra (-0.5%) came under pressure as CLSA and Goldman Sachs pointed to weak demand weighing on the medium-term outlook. On the flip side, Eternal (1.9%) and IndiGo (1.6%) posted the biggest gains.

### 2026-09-22 18:55:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

Treasury Yields Move Lower — The US 10-year Treasury yield fell nearly 2bps to 4.93% on Tuesday, reversing a modest early-session rise, as oil prices declined for a fifth straight session. Renewed hopes for a diplomatic solution to the conflict with Iran pushed oil down and helped ease concerns over inflationary pressures. Treasury yields have generally declined since the Fed raised rates last week for the first time since 2023, while Chair Warsh reaffirmed the central bank’s commitment to bring price stability, helping to restore its credibility. The decline in borrowing costs comes despite the Fed’s more hawkish tone, reinforced by recent comments from policymakers. Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee said the central bank cannot overlook persistent supply shocks, while St. Louis Fed President Alberto Musalem said further rate increases may be needed to bring inflation back toward target. Markets currently expect at least one more rate hike this year.

【英偉達市盈率降至十多年來最低 獲利力能否持久或遭市場質疑】英偉達的股票估值不斷下跌，正對這家晶片製造商能否維持其爆發性獲利增長的前景發出警告訊號。根據資料，英偉達目前的股價對應未來12個月預估獲利的市盈率已低於17倍，處於十多年來的最低便宜水平。這僅為2025年時的一半(當時英偉達的營收與獲利增速較慢)，且大幅低於今年5月創下的25倍以上預估市盈率。TCW主題股票和可持續增長股票高階投資組合經理Eli Horton表示，這表明市場對該公司目前獲利能力的永續性抱持著健康的懷疑態度。鑑於英偉達令人驚歎的基本面背景，其股價表現令人驚訝，但這也告訴你，市場預期低於目前的普遍共識預估。儘管英偉達股價在週一結束了連續五天的漲勢，但其折價估值依然存在。在此之前，半導體股出現了更廣泛的反彈。AI業領袖們稍早呼籲放慢最先進AI模型開發速度的言論嚇壞了投資人，導致費城半導體指數(SOX)在9月14日下跌近6%。

Ireland Wholesale Inflation Eases in August — Wholesale prices in Ireland rose 6.5% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 7.3% increase in July which was the highest since October 2022. Domestic prices increased 2.4%, while export producer prices rose 6.9%. Producer prices recorded notable year-on-year increases in chemicals and chemical products (16.5%), electrical equipment (6.1%), other non-metallic mineral products (6.0%), fabricated metal products excluding machinery and equipment (4.9%), and basic metals (4.8%). Food products rose 0.6%, driven by fish and fish products (8.6%) and other food products (5.8%), while meat and meat products fell 9.6%. Meanwhile, energy fuels surged 34.5%, electricity prices jumped 76.9%, and construction products rose 3.3%. On a monthly basis, wholesale prices fell 0.8% in August, reversing a 3.2% increase in July.

Gasoline Extends Decline — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.40 a gallon, extending losses for a second consecutive session, after reports that Iran proposed reopening Strait of Hormuz shipping within seven days, contingent on the US lifting its blockade. The statement bolstered hopes for a conflict resolution as President Trump is set to address the UN General Assembly on Tuesday, where Iranian President Pezeshkian will also attend. Fears over prolonged disruptions to Saudi Arabia's East-West pipeline also abated following reports the critical line is under testing and could partially restart this week. Meanwhile, the crack spread hit record highs as refining margins widened amid rising refined fuel prices. As winter approaches and heating demand rises, refiners are shifting output toward higher-priced diesel, tightening gasoline markets. Persistent Ukrainian attacks on Russian refining infrastructure have further supported elevated prices, with Russia set to extend its diesel export ban beyond September.

### 2026-09-22 18:09:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Factory Orders #Importance 2

UK Factory Orders Highest Since July 2023 — The UK’s total order book balance improved to -9 in September 2026 from -25 in August, marking the strongest reading since July 2023 and beating market expectations of -34. Export orders also improved, while remaining below “normal” at -9, but well above the historical average of -19. Output fell only marginally, with the balance improving sharply to -4 from -17. Manufacturers expect output to decline slightly again through December, at -6. Selling-price expectations also eased considerably to +12 from +22 in August, moving closer to the long-run average of +8 and suggesting some cooling in cost pressures. However, finished-goods inventories were only just adequate, with the balance falling to +2. The CBI said the data point to tentative stabilisation in manufacturing, but warned that high energy, employment and supply-chain costs continue to constrain firms. It said the upcoming Budget could help support investment and growth by reducing business costs and improving confidence.

### 2026-09-22 18:08:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Steel Declines from 2-Week High — Steel rebar futures fell to CNY 3,100 per tonne from the two-week high of CNY 3,130 on September 16th amid lower iron ore prices and downside risks on demand. The property crisis in top steel consumer China continued to prevent significant demand growth for rebar. Housing prices fell 3% annually in August as existing home sales took the majority of home sales in the country on a year-to-date basis, reflecting the government's effort to cap the oversupply of residential properties. The developments limit steel buying from major developers in the country, among the main buyers of rebar in the world. Consistently, the official construction PMI in China feel to a record low of 46.9. Elsewhere, a group of trade finance firms accused trader Radiant World of fraudulent invoices to back receivables, driving ore prices to fall sharply as trade finance liquidity dried.

【外交部：願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版】第23屆中國—東盟博覽會21日成功閉幕，外交部發言人郭嘉昆22日表示，中方願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版，攜手共建更為緊密的中國—東盟命運共同體。當日例行記者會上，有記者問：昨天，第23屆中國—東盟博覽會成功閉幕，取得不少新成果。上週，平陸運河正式通航受到國內外輿論高度關注。有評論稱，東博會“黃金平臺”和平陸運河“黃金通道”將開啟中國—東盟合作新篇章。發言人對此有何評論？郭嘉昆說，中國—東盟博覽會是雙方合作的“金色名片”。23年來，東博會見證中國與東盟從“黃金十年”到“鑽石十年”再到自貿區3.0版的升級跨越。本屆東博會首次設立“東盟國家需求展區”，首次釋出東盟國家需求清單，以“東盟出題、中國解題”新模式，推動供需精準對接。“本屆東博會還專門設立了平陸運河展區，運河通航後將大幅縮短中國西南地區到東盟國家的海運距離，開闢雙方貿易往來新通道。”郭嘉昆表示，“中方願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版，攜手共建更為緊密的中國—東盟命運共同體。”（新華社）

### 2026-09-22 17:58:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cotton Falls to One-Week Low — Cotton futures fell to around 82 cents per pound, its lowest level in little over a week, as traders assess whether demand can keep pace with the expected rise in new-crop availability. As the seasonal harvest progresses, additional supply is weighing on prices, particularly in Arizona, where 39% of the crop had been harvested, and Texas, up to 23%, in the week ending September 20th. Additionally, while high temperatures and lack of precipitation, particularly in Texas, increasing stress on crops, its conditions continue to worsen, with only 34% of crops rated good or excelent on the same week. Bearish sentiment was reinforced by weak export demand, as USDA's latest export sales report showed shipments declined to 71.2 thousand running bales for the week ended September 10th. Meanwhile, Brazilian output for 2025/26 is estimated to reach a record 4.5 million tons of lint, according to Agroconsult, while exports of Brazilian cotton surged 37.3% year-on-year in August.

Sterling Weakens to Near Two-Month Low — Sterling weakened to $1.336, near its lowest level since late July, as investors assessed the latest developments in the Middle East alongside weaker-than-expected UK public finances. The US dollar remained supported by expectations of further interest-rate hikes from the Federal Reserve. Brent crude resumed its recent decline after reports that Iran had proposed reopening the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if Washington agrees to demands including lifting its military blockade of Iranian ports. Meanwhile, UK government data showed the budget deficit widened to £18.3 billion in August, above the £15.5 billion forecast, partly reflecting higher inflation-linked spending on pensions and benefits. Attention is now turning to next month’s Autumn Budget, with the government reportedly considering changes to its fiscal rules that would allow borrowing for infrastructure projects to be excluded.

European Stocks Rebound — Stocks in Europe moved higher on Tuesday, with the STOXX 50 up 0.4% and the STOXX 600 gaining 0.3%, after a lacklustre open. Falling oil prices helped lift sentiment, as renewed hopes for a diplomatic solution to the conflict with Iran emerged. Kyodo News reported that Tehran had proposed reopening the Strait of Hormuz within seven days of the blockade being lifted, potentially providing a basis for talks during the UN General Assembly. US President Trump is due to address the assembly in New York and may meet Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian on the sidelines. Software and retail stocks were among the strongest performers. Prosus (5.5%) was among the top performers, Adidas as also gaining nearly 3% and Inditex went up 1.8%. In contrast, insurance and energy stocks were mostly in the red, including Allianz (-2%) and BP (-1.8%).

Meanwhile, ECB’s Chief Economist Philip Lane warned that a new wave of higher energy prices could keep Eurozone inflation elevated for longer than policymakers initially anticipated. Following this month’s second rate hike since the Iran war sent energy costs soaring, ECB officials have begun laying the groundwork for further tightening. Luxembourg Current Account Deficit Narrows in Q2 — Luxembourg’s current account deficit narrowed sharply to EUR 436.5 million in the second quarter of 2026 from EUR 1,482.8 million in the corresponding quarter of the previous year. The goods account surplus widened to EUR 327.7 million from EUR 152.8 million, while the services account surplus also increased to EUR 7,684.2 million from EUR 5,172.0 million in Q2 2025. On the other hand, the primary income deficit expanded to EUR 8,452.8 million from EUR 6,793.7 million, driven largely by wider deficits in both employee income (EUR 3,828.5 million vs EUR 3,676.7 million) and investment income (EUR 4,624.2 million vs EUR 3,116.9 million), while the secondary income deficit grew to EUR 20.1 million from EUR 13.9 million.

### 2026-09-22 17:34:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Morocco #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Morocco Posts Deflation for 2nd Month — Consumer prices in Morocco fell by 0.3% year-on-year in August 2026, following a 0.6% decrease in July, as deflation persisted for a second straight month. Food and non-alcoholic beverages posted a sharper price decline of 4.2% in August, compared with 4.0% in July, while communication prices fell 0.2% for a second month. Meanwhile, inflation moderated for housing and utilities (1.4% vs 1.5%) and restaurants and hotels (2.3% vs 2.4%). On the other hand, price growth accelerated sharply for transportation (7.3% vs 4.1%). The core inflation index, which excludes products with volatile prices and those subject to public tariffs, increased by 0.2% from July and by 0.1% from August 2025. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.8%, after a 1% decline in the previous month.

Wheat Falls as US Weather Improves, Black Sea Risks Persist — Wheat fell to around $7.2 per bushel, moving further below the 3-1/2-year high of $7.67 reached on August 28, as improving weather in parts of the US Plains eased concerns over winter wheat conditions. The USDA said recent rainfall across the northern and central Plains had improved topsoil moisture and prospects for germination, although planting remained behind average at 17% complete versus the five-year average of 21%. Kansas was 10% planted, Oklahoma 4% and Texas 21%. Meanwhile, continued attacks between Russia and Ukraine have clouded prospects for a recovery in Black Sea grain exports, with strikes on vessels and ports leaving seaborne shipments largely disrupted. Russia has increasingly redirected grain through Baltic and Arctic terminals, although volumes remain below normal Black Sea flows. Markets are also monitoring diplomatic efforts at the UN and the Trump-Xi meeting for potential developments affecting energy flows and Chinese agricultural purchases.

### 2026-09-22 17:15:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Rubber Futures Hold in Tight Range — Rubber futures held around 238 US cents per kilogram in late September, moving within a tight range after retreating from a 13-year high reached earlier this month. Immediate supply risks eased somewhat as rainfall in parts of Thailand was expected to improve, potentially supporting rubber tapping, although El Niño-related dryness remains a concern across much of Southeast Asia. Meanwhile, softer demand from China, the world's largest rubber consumer, also weighed on prices as weaker tire production and operating rates reduced raw-rubber purchases. Some manufacturers also plan holiday closures ahead of China's National Day and Mid-Autumn Festival, which could further reduce near-term rubber demand. In the meantime, crude oil prices decreased for the fifth day, making synthetic rubber cheaper and reducing the relative appeal of natural rubber.

### 2026-09-22 17:14:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Slovenia Jobless Rate Edges Up in July — Slovenia’s unemployment rate edged up to 4.4% in July 2026 from 4.3% in the previous month. The number of unemployed people increased by 1,012 from the previous month to 43,257, while declining by 542 year-on-year. Unemployment among those aged over 55 accounted for the largest share of unemployment (24.2%), followed by those aged 40–49 (23.9%) and those aged 30–39 (21.4%). Meanwhile, the share of unemployed people with tertiary education rose to 19.3% from 18.5% in the previous month, while first-time jobseekers accounted for 16.6%, down from 17.7%. The share of those unemployed for more than a year eased to 37.9% from 38.7% in June, while the share of those unemployed for more than two years also moderated to 20.9% from 21.6%.

### 2026-09-22 17:07:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palm Oil Holds at Elevated Level — Malaysian palm oil futures hovered above MYR 4,850 per tonne, holding their jump since mid-August on strong demand for vegetable oils. Biofuel feedstock costs saw a broad increase from palm oil and soy oil, with persistent risks of an escalation in the Middle East and concerns of lower crude oil output from Saudi Arabia lifting demand for biofuel alternatives. On top of that, Indonesia’s B50 biodiesel mandate is set to divert more palm oil to domestic use. Likewise, imports from top consumer India rose 7% from July to 782,761 tonnes in August, the highest since February, as refiners replenished stocks ahead of the festival season. Separately, Malaysia raised its October crude palm oil reference price while keeping the export duty at 10%. In turn, the forecast of one of the most aggressive El Ninos in history continued to threaten supply from major producers Malaysia and Indonesia.

Belgium Consumer Sentiment Falls Further in September — The consumer confidence indicator in Belgium fell to -10 in September 2026 from -7 in August, marking the seventh consecutive month of negative readings and the lowest level since May. Meanwhile, expectations for unemployment remained unchanged at 16, while perceptions of households’ financial situation were steady at -2 for a fourth consecutive month.

Slovenia Consumer Confidence Falls to 4-Month Low — The consumer confidence indicator in Slovenia fell by 1 percentage point month-on-month to -23 in September 2026 from -22 in the previous month. This marked the lowest reading in four months, as expectations regarding the economic situation for the past 12 months (-38 vs -37 in August) and the year ahead (-29 vs -27) worsened. Households’ assessments of their financial situation over the past 12 months (-19 vs -16) also deteriorated, while those for the year ahead improved slightly (-14 vs -15). Moreover, savings prospects over the next 12 months weakened (-14 vs -10). In contrast, willingness to make major purchases over the next 12 months improved (-31 vs -32). Among other indicators, price expectations for the past 12 months increased to 68 from 65, while those for the next 12 months rose to 61 from 56. Concerns about unemployment a year ahead eased, falling to 17 from 22.

Brent Extends Losses for 5th Session — Brent crude fell below $100 a barrel on Tuesday, extending losses into a fifth session and reaching the lowest level in two weeks, after reports suggested Iran could reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the US lifted its blockade of Iranian ports. Japan’s Kyodo News reported that Tehran had proposed reopening the strategic waterway within seven days of the blockade being lifted, potentially creating a basis for discussions with mediating countries during the UN General Assembly. President Donald Trump is due to address the assembly in New York and may meet Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian on the sidelines. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia has continued moving crude through Hormuz at around 2.9 million barrels per day over the past six days. Satellite imagery showed supertankers with combined capacity of about 14 million barrels at Saudi Gulf export terminals, the highest count observed since at least June.

### 2026-09-22 16:21:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Spain Trade Deficit Deepens in July — Spain’s trade deficit widened to €5.22 billion in July 2026 from €4.01 billion in the same period a year earlier. Imports rose 5.9% year-on-year to €40.68 billion, driven by higher purchases of vehicles and motorcycles (19.8%), energy products (13.4%), namely oil and derivatives (9.9%), electronic equipment (22.7%), and minerals (45.8%). In contrast, purchases of chemical products fell 2.8%. Among key trading partners, imports rose from China (9.9%), Algeria (61.7%), Italy (8.7%), and the UK (26.4%). Meanwhile, exports rose at a slower pace of 3% to €35.45 billion, supported by higher shipments of energy products (60.8%), particularly oil and derivatives (62.8%). Shipments of general-use equipment increased 16.5%, while exports of non-ferrous metals and minerals rose 22.2% and 26.2%, respectively. By destination, shipments were mainly directed to eurozone countries, particularly Italy (14.4%), Portugal (8.4%), and Germany (5.6%), as well as the US (19.2%).
